# docs/dev/measurements/2026-10-25-b142prep-census.txt -- [B142] (lane b142prep, inbox I-159)
#
# SOURCE: python3 docs/dev/measurements/probe_b142prep_emit.py EMITDIR   (compile-only emits, gzip'd, both pins + one deny arm)
#         python3 docs/dev/measurements/probe_b142prep_census.py EMITDIR OUT   (this file's rows). No match run, no gcc, no timing.
# POPULATION: 367 patterns of 10 sub-bench dirs' enumerated sets x {plain, whole-subject} x {auto-caps, auto-nocaps}
# = 1,468 rows (-e utf8 on the utf8 set; --features all), each emitted at 6b2dbd3e (abi 76, BEFORE) and 9ae59f5d4
# (abi 84), plus -fno-rev-end-onewalk at 9ae59f5d4. Sizes are pcrec's OWN (--warn-emit-bytes=1: `bytes:code`).
#
# IDENTITY. v2 (tools/program_identity.py, the record field's normalization) reads EVERY compiled-both row CHANGED: the find-all
# iterator scaffold (abi 83/84) is in every artifact and every exported residual entry became a forwarder to a static twin.
# v3 (normalize_v3, this lane) strips the scaffold, folds the twins back and sorts the top-level items; the columns v2 / v3
# are both given. v3 is a CENSUS-side normalization; the record field `program_sha256` stays v2 (OWED: a BD13 ruling).
#
# RESULT (v3):             plain 734 | whole 734
#   identical               662 | 677
#   changed                  25 | 11
#   refused-both             47 | 46
#   refusal movers            0 | 0   (no row compiles at one pin and not the other)
#   v2 identical              0 | 0   (of 1375 compiled-both rows)
#
# EVERY v3-CHANGED ROW IS ATTRIBUTED (the `attribution` column; `UNATTRIBUTED` count = 0):
#   abi78-rev-end-mark     18 rows
#   flag-spell(no-abi)     12 rows
#   abi77-compound         4 rows
#   abi83-k128             2 rows
#   abi78-rev-end-mark   18 rows = 9 patterns x {caps, nocaps}, plain form only: capability tail-digits-eol, tail-dotstar-txt, tail-ext-lower-txt,
#                        tail-space-eol, wild-semdiv-dollar-trailing-newline-pcre2; litrun ctrl-abc-dollar; sentinel wild-semdiv-dollar-trailing-newline-pcre2;
#                        syntax anc-dollar, anc-z-uc. The onewalk arm == 6b2dbd3e's program ('to-old') on all 18. Sizes SHRINK 1.0-3.4 KB.
#   abi77-compound      4 rows = capability + sentinel wild-waf-crs-942360-concat-sqli, WHOLE form, caps + nocaps: RX_COMPOUND `split`, 402,992 -> 516,567 B.
#   flag-spell(no-abi)  12 rows, all VM, patterns that START with an option run ((?x) (?i) (?J)): bracket-array-define x4, codegrammar-xflag x2,
#                        wild-secrets-slack-webhook-url x2, mod-j-uc x4. A dead label leaves the program (-50 B, vm_program_bytes -57 on (?i)abc):
#                        pcrec's [FLAG-SPELL] (flagfix), merged WITHOUT an abi bump -- NOT in I-159's list.
#   abi83-k128           2 rows = capability evil-alt-nested caps x {plain, whole}: `^(([a-z]+)*)+$` gains the last-mandatory-iteration guard (+333 B).
#
# SIZE BOOK (pcrec's own bytes, v3-identical compiled-both rows; engine x ITER_RETRY -> (d total, d code): rows):
#   ('dfa', 'none')          (106, 106)         1027
#   ('vm', 'none')           (106, 106)          216
#   ('vm', 'retry-budgeted') (106, 106)           14
#   ('dfa', 'retry')         (4085, 2405)         10
#   ('dfa', 'retry')         (60257, 2004)         8
#   ('dfa', 'none')          (115, 115)            8
#   ('dfa', 'retry')         (4081, 2401)          6
#   ('dfa', 'retry')         (4083, 2403)          6
#   ('dfa', 'retry')         (4079, 2399)          4
#   ('vm', 'none')           (104, 104)            4
#   ('dfa', 'retry')         (3064, 1456)          4
#   ('dfa', 'retry')         (4039, 2247)          3
#   => +106 B flat (the three new stamp lines + the .measures/.next/.next_n initializer lines) on every artifact whose ITER_RETRY is `none`
#      or VM `retry-budgeted`; a DFA artifact whose ITER_RETRY is `retry` ALSO carries the notempty-rooted retry MACHINE (it is a machine, so it
#      counts): +3,064..+4,128 B on the narrow DFAs, but it COPIES THE TABLES, so it scales with the machine: the bounded `cls-upto-N` WHOLE-subject
#      ladder grows +31,585 B (512), +60,257 (1024), +117,601 (2048), +237,081 B (4096) -- emit_bytes only (the code term moves +2.0 KB: tables are not code), and
#      the same +60,257 on grp-upto-1024 whole and capability letters-bounded-tail-z (plain AND whole). `retry` is 68 of 1,375 compiled-both rows (all DFA).
#      The 1,000,000 B max_emit_bytes cap sees the machine (artifact_size_term: scaffold is uncounted, the retry MACHINE is counted): no row crosses it at
#      the default caps (0 refusal movers), but a rung whose pre-abi-83 size was within 2x of the cap now is not.
#
# STAMPS THAT MOVED WITHOUT A PROGRAM MOVE (invisible to v2/v3 by construction -- a both-sided stamp whose value moved with no code change):
#   RX_VM_PROGRAM_BYTES                      14 rows
#   RX_ENGINE_WHY                            12 rows
#   RX_REQ_RUN                               8 rows
#   RX_DFA_MATCH,RX_DFA_PREFILTER,RX_DFA_PREFILTER_OFFSETS,RX_DFA_SCAN,RX_DFA_SCAN_EDGE,RX_DFA_START,RX_DFA_TABLE,RX_DFA_UNIFORM_FOLDS,RX_END_WINDOW,RX_REQ_HANDOFF,RX_REQ_WHY,RX_RUN_WORDS,RX_VM_START_SCAN 4 rows
#   RX_DFA_SCAN_EDGE                         2 rows
#   RX_VM_PREFILTER_LANG_WHY                 2 rows
#   RX_ENGINE_WHY (12 rows): the forcing construct NAMED changes (under --no-captures `\1` -> `\3` on capability phone-palindrome-6, tag-depth3-bound and `\1` -> `\2` on
#     syntax bak-2; `\1` -> `(?!...)` on capability quoted-delim-match; both configs `(?>...)` at pattern offset 14 -> 0 on wild-logparse-quotedstring-grok): the engine route
#     is unchanged (a diagnostic text, not a program move). RX_REQ_RUN (8 rows, syntax mod-reset and mod-unset, every form/config): `6174@0` -> `436174@0/dfffff` (the
#     necessary run grows from `at` to a caseless `Cat`; v3-identical, so the pre-check is not in the emitted text). The 4 compound rows move every machine-level stamp
#     to `compound` (`RX_DFA_UNIFORM_FOLDS` and `RX_RUN_WORDS` to -1) and add the parts' own `RX__F_*` / `RX__Q_*` families.
#
